# fast-mcp-scala public API baseline — for the 1.1.0 MiMa gate (ROADMAP.md 1.1.0 "MiMa gate")
# Generated 2026-09-08 by R3 step 1 (role publisher) from the release tree tjc-2277-release-prep @ 6fce1f9 (PR #99; contains main 7da60a4):
#   jar   : the R3 rehearsal `./mill --no-server __.publishLocal` of this tree (JVM artifact fast-mcp-scala_3.jar, ivy2Local, Scala 3.9.0,
#           built WITHOUT -experimental — PR #98 dropped the flag, so no @experimental taint reaches TASTy)
#   source: this tree (shared/src + jvm/src) for the public/private split of top-level decls
#   method: identical to the v0 INPUT (C1 step 4, commit cad9b60): sorted source-public top-level types, jar top-level class files,
#           `javap -public` of the root-import export surface
# Supersedes the v0 INPUT generated from main 8b2fede. Deltas now IN the jar (fix PRs #96-#107 merged to main; PR B's own removals on this tree):
#   - PR B (prep) deletes the RC1/RC4-deprecated ErrorCodes.ResourceNotFound, ElicitRequestUrlParams.requiredError, HostGuard.isAllowed/3-arg
#   - PR #102 (+ follow-up) widens the root-import export list; adds vs v0: BlobResourceContents, ClientCapabilities, CompleteRequestParams, Completion, CompletionArgument, CompletionContext, CompletionReference, CreateMessageRequestParams, CreateMessageResult, ElicitRequestParams, ElicitRequestUrlParams, ElicitResult, Implementation, LimitSettings, ListRootsResult, LoggingLevel, ModelHint, ModelPreferences, ProgressToken, PromptReference, ResourceContents, ResourceTemplateReference, Root, SamplingMessage, TaskOwnerKey, TaskSettings, TaskSupport, TextResourceContents, ToolChoice
#   - PR #102 removes McpServerSettings ctor params debug, logLevel, warnOnDuplicate{Resources,Tools,Prompts}, dependencies and McpServer.dependencies
#   - PR #103 macro defaults / @Param Option semantics; PR #98 no @experimental (bytecode-invisible to javap; TASTy-visible to MiMa's Scala 3 checks)
# Sections:
#   TYPE       <fqcn> <kind> <src>            top-level, source-public (non-private) decl; jar=yes|no (type aliases have no class file)
#   INTERNAL   <fqcn>                          class file in the jar whose top-level decl is private[...] or nested on this tree
#                                              (bytecode-public only) -> pre-seed the MiMa exclude list with these
#   TYPE-HEADER/MEMBER <class> <javap -public line>  members of the ROOT-IMPORT export surface (Exports.scala + ExportsJvm.scala,
#                                              class + companion `$`, plus the Exports$package$ forwarders and Types$package$ extensions)
# Policy inputs (S1.4.7): com.tjclp.fastmcp.examples.* and com.tjclp.fastmcp.macros.* are proposed OUTSIDE the 1.x compatibility contract.
